Gradient topK implementation vectorized per top:
```
def _active_gradient(x, c, top_k, weight_beta):
    n=len(x)
    if top_k >= len(c): top_k=len(c)
    # find top indices
    if top_k == len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c))
    else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]  # not sorted maybe
    vals=c[idx]
    w=np.exp((vals-vals.max())*weight_beta) # Beta dimensionless with vals/cmax? Use temp
    w/=w.sum()
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for idx_i, wi in zip(idx,w):
        if wi ==0: continue
        k=int(idx_i)
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        # mirror slice
        grad[lo:hi+1] += wi * x[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]
    return grad
```
Need check step when step -1 and stop. For k<n: hi=k, lo=0, stop=k-hi-1=-1, slice x[k:-1:-1] good. For k>=n: hi=n-1, stop=k-hi-1 = k-n, slice x[n-1:k-n:-1]. If k-n=0? for k=n? slice x[n-1:0:-1] excludes 0, length n-1; but valid j=1..n-1? For k=n, lo=1, hi=n-1; mirror indices n-1 down to1; x[0] not included, correct since k-j for j=0 =n invalid. stop=k-n=0, slice stop 0 exclusive: x[n-1:0:-1] length n-1. Good. If k=2n-2: lo=n-1,hi=n-1, slice x[n-1:n-1:-1]? stop=0? k-hi-1=2n-3-(n-1)-1? compute hi=n-1, stop=k-hi-1=2n-2-n+1-1=n-2? slice x[n-1:n-2:-1] length1 x[n-1]. Good.

def _run_mixed(x, deadline, name, target_score, start_B=0.5, end_B=50, ...):
    n=len(x); L=2*n-1; N=pow2(L); avg=1.0/L
    x=x.copy(); x=x/sum(x)
    momentum=zeros
    best=x.copy(); best_cmax=inf
    current_x=x
    beta=0.9; lr=0.15; B=start_B
    step=0
    target_c = target_score/(2*n)
    # maybe if current score <best set
    while time<deadline:
        if step % update_B_interval: B = min(end_B, B*1.05)
        X=fft(x,N); c=ifft(X*X).real[:L]; cmax=c.max()
        score=2*n*cmax
        if score<best_score-1e-12: best...
        # choose spectral? if step % spec_interval == 0:
           if spectral:
               current_cap = max(target_c, cmax - lr_spec* (cmax-avg))  # lr_spec anneal 0.02->0.5
               T=cap_target(c, cap, avg)
               # try candidate with mu=0.5
               cand, ok = spectral_candidate(x, X, T, N)
               if ok:
                   score_c = fast score cand via maybe ifft(FFT(cand)^2)
                   if score_c < score:
                       x = (1-mu)*x + mu*cand? Wait cand already blended? Let's have cand not blended, then blend.
                       # Use mu values
                   # Update cap_lr if improved/increased
        # gradient step
        grad = active_gradient(x,c,top_k,B)
        g = grad - mean
        momentum = ...
        scale = max(|momentum|)
        rel = lr * momentum / scale
        x = x * exp(-rel)
        x = max(x, eps); x/=sum
        step++
        # if no improve for N: reheat/adjust
```

Need implement `cap_target`:
```
def _cap_target(c, cap):
    L=len(c); avg=1/L
    if cap <=0: return np.full(L, avg)
    red = np.maximum(c - cap, 0)
    total_red = red.sum()
    if total_red > 0:
        T = c - red + total_red/L
    else:
        T = c.copy()
    # ensure sum 1: T/=T.sum()
    # If T sum not 1 due numerical, adjust
    if T.sum() >0: T=T/T.sum()
    return T
```
If total_red/L huge and T values become negative? c-red ≥0; add positive -> nonnegative.

Flow:
1. `x_parent = normalize(parent)`. `best = parent`, `best_score`.
2. Run `_run_mixed(x_parent, deadline1)` with deadline = t0 + 0.6*budget. It returns improved best and local.
3. For some number of restarts (3-5), take current best, add scaled random noise or use alternative initializations, run `_run_mixed` with short budget each (e.g. 60-120s) if improves. 
4. If time left, try alt length maybe 8000 with initialized from best resampled plus random, budget remaining*0.5? Then final evaluate.

       g = grad - grad.mean()

       maxg = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if maxg < 1e-14:
           # random small
       else:
           momentum = beta_mom*momentum + (1-beta_mom)*g
           maxv=np.max(np.abs(momentum)); 
           rel = (lr_current / max(maxv,1e-12)) * momentum
           # To prevent large, cap rel e.g. [-2,2]
           rel = np.clip(rel, -2.0, 2.0)

           s=x.sum(); if s<1e-12: x=best; else x/=s

Spectral `_spectral_candidate` implementation details:
```
def _spectral_candidate(x, X, C_target, N):
    n = len(x)
    L=2*n-1
    Ct = fft(C_target, N)
    tol = 1e-10 * (np.max(np.abs(X)) + 1.0)
    Y = np.empty_like(X)
    mask = np.abs(X) > tol
    # For masked, use 0.5*(X + Ct/X)
    Y[mask] = 0.5 * (X[mask] + Ct[mask] / X[mask])
    # For unmasked, maybe keep X to avoid blow; or 0.5*X
    Y[~mask] = X[~mask]
    trial = np.real(ifft(Y))
    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
    s=trial.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    trial /= s
    # Optional: if negative clipping removed too much, blend with old? 
    return trial
```
This uses X from current x. But if we accept and next step, x changed. Good.

def _blend_trial(x, trial, mu): return (1-mu)*x + mu*trial normalized

Resample function:
```
def _resample(a, n):
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    old=np.arange(m)
    new=np.linspace(0, m-1, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)
```
Normalize after.

def _lp_improve(x, deadline, max_iter, top=300, verbose=True):
    n=len(x); L=2n-1; N=pow2(L)
    x=sum1; best=x; best_score
    reduction=0.005
    t=0.25
    for i... while time:
       c=fft conv; cmax; score
       update best
       if score <= target: maybe lower reduction
       M_val = cmax*(1-reduction)
       selected = top indices of c maybe min(top, max(50, int(top)))
       # Try LP with M_val; if infeasible/unsuccessful, try M=cmax
       g=lp_solve(...)
       if g is not None:
           gs=sum(g); if gs<...; g=g/gs
           for mu in [t, 0.5*t, 0.1,0.05]:
               y=(1-mu)*x + mu*g; normalize; score_y; if score_y<score: accept; set t=mu; if score_y < score*0.999? break
           if no accept: reduction *=0.5; maybe skip
       else: reduction*=0.3
       if accepted: reduction = min(0.05, reduction*1.1)? maybe increase slowly
    return best
```

LP solver in code:
```
def _solve_lp(g_coeff, M, selected, f, U, time_limit=2.0):
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    n=len(f)
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(selected):
       lo...; fvals = f[lo?]. Need mapping f index = k-j.
       # fvals length m: for j=lo to hi, idx=k-j => k-lo down to k-hi.
       data.extend(fidx_values)
       cols.extend(range(lo,hi+1))
       rows.extend([r]*m)
    A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(len(selected), n))
    c = -np.ones(n)
    b = np.full(len(selected), M)
    sol = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='HiGHS', options={'time_limit': time_limit})
    if sol.success or sol.status in (0,3?)...
       return sol.x
```
Bounds list of tuples large (n=5582) okay. Use `bounds=(0, U)`? SciPy linprog accepts `bounds` as tuple for all variables? Yes: `bounds=(0, U)` sets for all. Great.

Inside each iteration:
- if time near deadline - 10 break.

- if cmax already <= target_coeff*1.001, maybe reduction smaller.

- If LP infeasible, use M=cmax.
- Solve. If fails, reduction *=0.5.
- Success: g. Normalize. Try blend `gamma` in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]. For each compute score; accept first improving. To compute score, FFT. If no improve, maybe set gamma=0 (skip) and reduction*=0.8.
- If accepted: update x; reduction = min(0.08, reduction*1.02+0.001); gamma maybe last. If no accept: reduction *=0.7; if reduction<1e-4 set 0.002.
- Track best.
This may require many FFTs: score of each gamma (up to4) plus current c. Limit gamma trials to 2 [0.2,0.05]. If cmax after blend > current, skip.

It will find some g. If QP? okay. Updating with g may not reduce max.

"""Docstring..."""
import time

No lambdas. Helper top-level.

Functions:
- `_pow2(n)`
- `_normalize_sum1(x)` returns array copy, handles invalid.
- `_score_fft(x, N=None)` maybe compute c and score. But need X. We can have `_fft_self(x,N)` returns X,c.
```
def _self_conv_fft(x, N=None):
    n=len(x); L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=_pow2(L)
    X=fft(x,N)
    c=ifft(X*X).real[:L]
    return X,c
```
- `_fast_score(x,N=None)`: call self_conv, return 2*n*max(c)
- `_active_gradient(x,c,top_k,B)`: as above.
- `_cap_target(c,cap)`
- `_spectral_candidate(x,X,C_target,N)`
- `_lp_step(x,c, selected, M, rng, U, time_limit)` maybe returns g or None.
- `_lp_improve(x, deadline, target_score, top_rows, random_rows, time_limit, rng)`
- `_run_mixed(x, deadline, target_score, B_start, B_end, top_k, beta, lr, spec_interval, spec_max_red, rng)`
- `_resample(a,n)`
- `_safe_score_actual(seq)`: call evaluate_sequence if defined; return float or inf. But don't import. In function:

def _actual_score(seq):

`_run_mixed` details code:
```
def _run_mixed(x, deadline, target_score=1.45, B_start=0.5, B_end=60.0, top_k=8, beta=0.85, lr=0.15, spec_interval=200, spec_max_red=0.6, min_lr=0.02, max_lr=0.6, rng=None, verbose=True):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    n=...
    x=_normalize_sum1(x)
    N=_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=x.copy(); best_score=inf; current_score=inf
    momentum=zeros
    step=0; B=float(B_start); spec_red=0.02; stuck=0
    target_coeff = max(target_score/(2.0*n), 1.0/(2*n)) # >=avg
    lr_cur=lr
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        X,c=_self_conv_fft(x,N)
        cmax=float(c.max())
        score=2.0*n*cmax
        if score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_score=score; best=x.copy(); stuck=0; lr_cur=min(max_lr, lr_cur*1.01)
            if verbose and (step%200==0): print
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck % 20 == 0:
                lr_cur=max(min_lr, lr_cur*0.98)
        if stuck > 800:
            # reheat from best
            x = best.copy()
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.05 / np.sqrt(n) # absolute small? Best sum1 so std ~1/sqrt(n); absolute 0.05/sqrt(n) is 5% of typical
            x = x + noise
            x = np.maximum(x,0); x/=x.sum()
            momentum=zeros; B=max(B_start,B*0.8); spec_red=0.02; stuck=0
        # spectral
        if step > 20 and step % spec_interval == 0 and cmax > target_coeff*(1.0+1e-8):
             cap = max(target_coeff, cmax - spec_red*(cmax - 1.0/(2*n)))
             T = _cap_target(c, cap)
             cand = _spectral_candidate(x,X,T,N)
             if cand is not None:
                 # eval cand
                 _, cc = _self_conv_fft(cand,N)
                 ccmax=float(cc.max()); sc=2*n*ccmax
                 accepted=False
                 if sc < score - 1e-12:
                     x=cand.copy(); spec_red=min(spec_max_red, spec_red*1.3); accepted=True
                 else:
                     # try blend
                     blend=(0.7*x+0.3*cand); blend=_normalize_sum1(blend)
                     _, cb=_self_conv_fft(blend,N); cbmax=float(cb.max()); sb=2*n*cbmax
                     if sb < score - 1e-12:
                         x=blend.copy(); spec_red=min(spec_max_red, spec_red*1.15); accepted=True
                     else:
                         spec_red=max(0.005, spec_red*0.8)
                 if accepted:
                     # maybe continue to next step
        # B anneal
        if step % 50 == 0:
            B = min(B_end, B*1.08)
        # gradient
        grad = _active_gradient(x,c,top_k,B)
        g = grad - grad.mean()
        maxg=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if maxg > 1e-16:
            momentum = beta*momentum + (1.0-beta)*g
            momentum = momentum - momentum.mean()
            maxv=np.max(np.abs(momentum))
            if maxv > 1e-16:
                rel = (lr_cur / maxv) * momentum
                rel = np.clip(rel, -1.0, 1.0)
                x = x * np.exp(-rel)
                x = np.maximum(x, 1e-15)
                s=x.sum()
                if s>1e-12: x/=s
                else: x=best.copy()
        step += 1
    return best, best_score, x
```
Potential: `spec_red*(cmax - avg)` with cmax and avg both ~1/n; okay. If cmax near target, cap≈target. If `cmax - avg` zero? no.
B update: B start0.5, factor1.08 every50 -> after 5000 steps huge. Maybe too fast. Use if B_start small. We can pass B end. 
top_k maybe 8. If B high, gradient near exact.
Stuck reheat: adding absolute noise `0.05/sqrt(n)`; for n=5582 std 0.0002, sum= sqrt(n)*0.05/√n=0.05? Good relative. Use `0.1/np.sqrt(n)` for stronger. Ensure after normalize.

def _lp_improve(x, deadline, target_score=1.45, top_rows=250, random_rows=150, time_limit=4.0, rng=None, verbose=True):
    if not _HAVE_LP: return x, inf? maybe score? Need return current score. 
    n, N
    best... current...
    reduction=0.01; gamma=0.25
    target_coeff=max(target_score/(2n), 1/(2n))
    while time < deadline:
       X,c=_self...; cmax; score; update best
       if cmax <= target_coeff*1.001: reduction=min(reduction,0.005)
       # selected
       top = top_rows - random_rows? Let's do: 
          n_top = max(20, min(top_rows, len(c)//2))
          top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -n_top)[-n_top:]
          if random_rows:
             rand_idx = rng.integers(0,len(c), size=min(random_rows,len(c)-len(set top)?))
          selected = np.unique(concat)
       M = max(target_coeff, cmax*(1-reduction))
       # if M < cmax? yes.
       g=None
       U = max(0.05, 10.0*float(np.max(x)) + 1e-3)
       # Could if U too low infeasible; try up to 1.0
       for try_U in [U, min(1.0, 5*U), 1.0]:
           g = _lp_solve(x,c,selected,M,try_U,time_limit)
           if g is not None: break
       if g is None:
           reduction *=0.7
           continue
       gsum=g.sum(); if gsum<1e-15: continue
       g/=gsum
       # try gammas
       accepted=False
       for gm in [gamma, 0.5*gamma, 0.1, 0.03]:
           y=(1-gm)*x+gm*g; y=_normalize_sum1(y)
           _, cy=_self_conv_fft(y,N); cy_max; sy=2n*...
           if sy < score - 1e-12:
               x=y; accepted=True; gamma=gm; reduction=min(0.05, reduction*1.05+0.001); break
       if not accepted:
           reduction *=0.8
           gamma=max(0.05,gamma*0.8)
       if time near break
    return best,best_score,x

Detailed code for helpers:

```
def _pow2(n):
    if n <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
```
If n int? pass int.

```
def _clean_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    return arr
```

```
def _normalize_sum1(a):
    arr=_clean_array(a)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-20:
       arr=np.full(arr.size,1.0/arr.size)
    else:
       arr=arr/s
    return arr
```

`_self_conv_fft`:
```
def _self_conv_fft(x,N=None):
    n=x.size; L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=_pow2(L)
    X=fft(x,N)
    c=ifft(X*X).real[:L]
    # remove tiny negative? c can have -1e-16; when max okay. For gradient, negative c maybe not top. Could `c=np.maximum(c,0.0)`? Convolution of nonnegative should nonnegative; numerical negative small. Clipping to 0 safe and may avoid weights. Use c[c<0 & small]=0? `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)` to be safe. But if numerical positive small, fine. Clipping may create exact nonnegative. Do it.
    return X,c
```

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(x,N=None):
    n=x.size
    _,c=_self_conv_fft(x,N)
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
```

`_active_gradient`: need if c contains NaN? no.
```
def _active_gradient(x,c,top_k,B):
    n=x.size; L=c.size
    if top_k <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if top_k >= L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    vals=c[idx]
    vmax=float(np.max(vals))
    if vmax <= 1e-300:
       w=np.full(len(idx),1.0/len(idx))
    else:
       # exponent = (vals-vmax)*(B / vmax)  # B dimensionless
       # If B large, exp underflow okay.
       exp_vals=np.exp((vals-vmax)*(B/vmax))
       sw=exp_vals.sum()
       if sw <= 0:
           w=np.full(len(idx),1.0/len(idx))
       else: w=exp_vals/sw
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for k,wi in zip(idx,w):
        if wi <= 0: continue
        intk=int(k)
        lo = 0 if intk < n-1 else intk - n + 1
        hi = intk if intk < n else n-1
        # Actually formulas lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(n-1,k)
        # Use int.
        mirror=x[intk-lo:intk-hi-1:-1]
        grad[lo:hi+1] += wi * mirror
    return grad
```
Check `lo` when intk=2n-2: n-1; hi=n-1. mirror x[n-1 - (n-1)=0? intk-lo = n-1; intk-hi-1 = n-2; step -1 -> [x[n-1]]]. Good.
For intk=n-1: lo=n-1 - n +1 =0; hi=n-1; mirror x[n-1:-1:-1] all? slice x[n-1:-1:-1] includes down to 0? Stop -1 exclusive -> yes.
For intk=0: lo=0,hi=0,mirror x[0:-1:-1] [x0]. Good.
`lo` formula: if intk - n +1 <0 -> 0. Use max to avoid negative. `lo = max(0, intk - n + 1); hi = min(n-1, intk)`. Then mirror slice `x[intk-lo:intk-hi-1:-1]`. For k=0: start0 stop -1, good. For k=2n-2: start n-1 stop n-2? intk-hi-1 = n-2? Yes. If hi=n-1, stop=n-2, good. If k=n: lo=1,hi=n-1,start=n-1, stop=0, slice x[n-1:0:-1] (length n-1). good. If k=n-1: lo=0,hi=n-1,start=n-1,stop=-1, length n. good.

Need ensure `idx` from argpartition dtype int64; int(k). Good.

Potential performance: loop over top K, each slice allocation? Slicing creates view; multiplication allocates length. For K=8,n=5582 -> okay. 
If B/vmax huge and vmax tiny maybe overflow in B/vmax? vmax ~1/n, B=100 => B/vmax ~100n ~5e5, exponent differences ≤5e5; exp(<=-large) underflows to0, okay. But B/vmax may overflow if vmax 1e-300; we check.

`_cap_target`:
```
def _cap_target(c, cap):
    L=c.size
    if cap <= 0:
       return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    red=np.maximum(c-cap,0.0)
    total=red.sum()
    if total <= 1e-18:
       T=c.copy()
    else:
       T=c - red + total/L
    s=T.sum()
    if s >0:
       T=T/s
    else: T=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    return T
```
For nonnegative, s=1 after normalization. If L even? okay.

`_spectral_candidate`:
```
def _spectral_candidate(x,X,C_target,N):
    n=x.size
    if C_target.size != 2*n-1: return None
    Ct=fft(C_target,N)
    absX=np.abs(X)
    maxabs=float(np.max(absX)) if absX.size else 0.0
    tol=1e-10*max(1.0, maxabs)
    Y=np.zeros_like(X, dtype=np.complex128)
    mask=absX>tol
    # vectorized
    Y[mask]=0.5*(X[mask] + Ct[mask]/X[mask])
    Y[~mask]=X[~mask]
    trial=np.real(ifft(Y))
    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
    s=float(trial.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
       return None
    trial=trial/s
    return trial
```
If Ct/X large causing trial huge, normalize. Could have `~mask` very frequent? X DC huge; others maybe. okay.

`_lp_solve`:
```
def _lp_solve(f, selected, M, U, time_limit=2.0):
    if not _HAVE_LP: return None
    n=f.size
    if selected.size < 5: return None
    row_parts=[]; col_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(selected):
       intk=int(k)
       lo=max(0,intk-n+1); hi=min(n-1,intk); m=hi-lo+1
       if m <=0: continue
       row_parts.append(np.full(m, r, dtype=np.int32))
       col_parts.append(np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=np.int32))
       data_parts.append(f[intk-lo:intk-hi-1:-1])
    if not row_parts: return None
    rows=np.concatenate(row_parts); cols=np.concatenate(col_parts); data=np.concatenate(data_parts).astype(np.float64)
    A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(selected.size,n))
    cvec=np.full(n, -1.0)
    bvec=np.full(selected.size, float(M))
    try:
       sol=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, float(U)), method='HiGHS', options={'time_limit': float(time_limit)})
    except TypeError:
       try:
          sol=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, float(U)))
       except Exception:
          return None
    except Exception:
       return None
    if sol is None or not hasattr(sol,'x') or sol.x is None: return None
    xg=np.asarray(sol.x,dtype=np.float64)
    # Accept if status 0 or 3? 
    status=getattr(sol,'status',999)
    if status not in (0,3): return None
    xg=np.maximum(xg,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(xg)): return None
    return xg
```
`bounds=(0.0,float(U))` should work. If U is 0? no. `selected.size` rows. If solver presolve warns, ignore. 
Potential: HiGHS time limit option maybe not recognized; if ValueError? We caught TypeError? Use generic except and retry without options.
```
try: sol=linprog(..., options={'time_limit':time_limit})
except Exception:
   try: sol=linprog(...)
   except: return None
```
If method unavailable, first exception maybe. Could first try method='HiGHS', if fails use default. Use `method='highs'` lowercase? SciPy accepts 'HiGHS'. We'll catch.

```
def _lp_improve(x, deadline, target_score=1.42, top_rows=180, random_rows=120, time_limit=3.0, rng=None, max_iters=None):
    if not _HAVE_LP:
        return x, _fast_score(x), x
    n=x.size; L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(L)
    x=_normalize_sum1(x)
    best=x.copy(); best_score=np.inf
    reduction=0.005; gamma=0.25; iter=0
    target_coeff=max(target_score/(2.0*n), 1.0/(2.0*n))
    # cap rows
    max_rows=max(30, min(top_rows+random_rows, int(1_500_000 // max(1,n))))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if max_iters and iter>=max_iters: break
       X,c=_self_conv_fft(x,N)
       cmax=float(c.max()); score=2*n*cmax
       if score < best_score -1e-10:
           best_score=score; best=x.copy()
       # if cmax already <= target_coeff: keep gentle
       base_max = float(np.max(x))
       n_top = max(20, min(max_rows, L//2))
       if n_top:
          top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -n_top)[-n_top:]
       else: top_idx=np.array([],dtype=int)
       n_rand = max(0, min(max_rows - len(np.unique(top_idx)), L//10? maybe))
```

   `n_top = max(20, int(max_rows*0.6))`

   top_idx argpartition; rand_idx = rng.integers(0,L,size=n_rand)
   selected = np.unique(np.concatenate([top_idx,rand_idx]))
   if selected.size<20: continue
       selected[:]=selected.astype(int)
       # M
       if cmax > target_coeff:
            M = max(target_coeff, cmax*(1-reduction))
       else:
            M = cmax*0.99? to allow improvement? If score below target, try cmax*(1-0.005). Use M=max(avg*1.001, cmax*(1-0.005))
       U_base=max(0.05, 10*base_max +1e-4)
       g=None
       for U_try in [U_base, min(1.0, U_base*5), 1.0]:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
           g=_lp_solve(x, selected, M, U_try, time_limit)
           if g is not None and g.sum()>1e-10: break
       if g is None:
           reduction=max(0.001, reduction*0.6)
           gamma=max(0.05, gamma*0.9)
           iter += 1; continue
       g=np.maximum(g,0); gs=g.sum(); if gs<...; g/=gs
       accepted=False
       for gm in [gamma, 0.5*gamma, 0.1, 0.03]:
           y=(1-gm)*x + gm*g
           y=_normalize_sum1(y)
           _, cy=_self_conv_fft(y,N)
           cy_max=float(cy.max()); sy=2*n*cy_max
           if sy < score - 1e-10:
                x=y.copy(); accepted=True; gamma=gm; break
       if accepted:
           reduction=min(0.04, reduction*1.05 +0.0005) # slow increase
       else:
           reduction=max(0.001, reduction*0.7)
           gamma=max(0.05, gamma*0.8)
       iter +=1
    # Ensure best maybe last? return best
    return best,best_score,x
```

    if isinstance(v, (int,float)) and np.isfinite(v): return float(v)

```python
"""
Non-negative step-function coefficient search.

Algorithm:
- Work with sum-1 nonnegative vectors. The objective is scale invariant:
  score = 2*n*max(conv(x,x)).
- Use FFT-based self-convolution for fast internal scoring.
- Alternatively combine:
  1) Sparse LP meta-iterations inspired by the previous active-set method:
       for fixed x, solve a sparse LP maximizing sum(g) subject to x*g <= M
       on a subset of important lags, then move toward g.
  2) Smooth/multiplicative gradient descent using the active top convolution
     peaks, with adaptive annealing and reheats.
  3) A spectral polynomial-square Newton "equalization" step that pushes
     conv(x,x) toward a capped/flat target.
- The parent sequence height_sequence_1 is used as the main starting point,
  with diversified perturubations and, if time allows, a resampled longer length.
- The best internal sequence is finally checked with the real evaluator and
  returned, choosing the better of the best and parent if needed.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    csr_matrix = None
    linprog = None
    _HAVE_LP = False


def _pow2(n):
    """Smallest power of two >= n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_array(a):
    """1D nonnegative finite float array."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    return arr


def _normalize_sum1(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, keeping nonnegative finite values."""
    arr = _clean_array(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-20:
        arr = np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def _resample(a, n):
    """Linearly resample 1D array to length n."""
    a = _clean_array(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, n)
    out = np.interp(new, old, a)
    return _normalize_sum1(out)


def _self_conv_fft(x, N=None):
    """Zero-padded linear self-convolution via FFT. Returns (X, c)."""
    n = int(x.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _pow2(L)
    X = fft(x, N)
    c = ifft(X * X).real[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return X, c


def _fast_score(x, N=None):
    """Internal FFT score for sum-1 x."""
    n = int(x.size)
    _, c = _self_conv_fft(x, N)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _active_gradient(x, c, top_k, B):
    """
    Weighted subgradient of max active convolution coefficients.

    For one lag s, d/dx_j C_s is proportional to x_{s-j}.
    Top lags are weighted by exp(B*(c_s - c_max)/c_max).
    """
    n = int(x.size)
    L = int(c.size)
    if top_k <= 0 or n == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if top_k >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:].astype(np.int64)
    vals = c[idx]
    vmax = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if vmax <= 1e-300:
        w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / max(1, idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        exponent = (vals - vmax) * (float(B) / vmax)
        ew = np.exp(exponent)
        sw = float(ew.sum())
        if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
            w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / max(1, idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = ew / sw

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, wi in zip(idx, w):
        if wi <= 0.0:
            continue
        intk = int(k)
        lo = max(0, intk - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, intk)
        # Mirror slice x[intk-lo : intk-hi-1 : -1]
        mirror = x[intk - lo:intk - hi - 1:-1]
        grad[lo:hi + 1] += wi * mirror
    return grad


def _cap_target(c, cap):
    """
    Build a nonnegative target convolution by shaving peaks above cap and
    redistributing the removed mass evenly, preserving total mass.
    """
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    flat = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    cap = float(cap)
    if cap <= 0.0:
        return flat
    red = np.maximum(c - cap, 0.0)
    total_red = float(red.sum())
    if total_red <= 1e-18:
        T = c.copy()
    else:
        T = c - red + total_red / float(L)
    s = float(T.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return flat
    T = T / s
    T = np.where(np.isfinite(T), T, 0.0)
    T = np.maximum(T, 0.0)
    s2 = float(T.sum())
    if s2 <= 0.0:
        return flat
    return T / s2


def _spectral_candidate(x, X, C_target, N):
    """
    Polynomial square-root Newton step for x*x ~= C_target.
    Keeps phase of current X, amplitudes from target FFT.
    Returns nonnegative sum-1 candidate or None.
    """
    n = int(x.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if C_target.size != L:
        return None
    Ct = fft(C_target, N)
    absX = np.abs(X)
    maxabs = float(np.max(absX)) if absX.size else 0.0
    tol = 1e-10 * max(1.0, maxabs)
    Y = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=np.complex128)
    mask = absX > tol
    if np.any(mask):
        Y[mask] = 0.5 * (X[mask] + Ct[mask] / X[mask])
    if np.any(~mask):
        Y[~mask] = X[~mask]
    trial = np.real(ifft(Y))
    trial = np.where(np.isfinite(trial), trial, 0.0)
    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
    s = float(trial.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return trial / s


def _lp_solve(f, selected, M, U, time_limit=3.0):
    """
    Sparse LP: maximize sum(g) subject to (f*g)[k] <= M for selected lags k,
    0 <= g <= U. Returns g or None.
    """
    if not _HAVE_LP:
        return None
    n = int(f.size)
    selected = np.asarray(selected, dtype=np.int64)
    if selected.size < 5 or n <= 0:
        return None

    row_parts = []
    col_parts = []
    data_parts = []

    for r, k in enumerate(selected):
        intk = int(k)
        lo = max(0, intk - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, intk)
        m = hi - lo + 1
        if m <= 0:
            continue
        row_parts.append(np.full(m, r, dtype=np.int32))
        col_parts.append(np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int32))
        data_parts.append(np.ascontiguousarray(f[intk - lo:intk - hi - 1:-1], dtype=np.float64))

    if not row_parts:
        return None

    rows = np.concatenate(row_parts)
    cols = np.concatenate(col_parts)
    data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
    A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(int(selected.size), n))

    cvec = np.full(n, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    bvec = np.full(selected.size, float(M), dtype=np.float64)

    sol = None
    # Prefer HiGHS, then default. Keep options minimal and robust.
    try:
        sol = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
                      method="HiGHS", options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)})
    except Exception:
        try:
            sol = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, float(U)),
                          options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)})
        except Exception:
            try:
                sol = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0.0, float(U)))
            except Exception:
                sol = None

    if sol is None or not hasattr(sol, "x") or sol.x is None:
        return None

    status = getattr(sol, "status", 999)
    # Accept optimal or a completed time-limited incumbent if present.
    if status not in (0, 3):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(sol.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    if float(g.sum()) <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return g


def _lp_improve(x, deadline, target_score=1.45, top_rows=180, random_rows=120,
                time_limit=3.0, rng=None, max_iters=200, verbose=True):
    """
    LP-based meta-optimization. Returns (best_x, best_score, last_x).
    """
    x = _normalize_sum1(x)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    n = int(x.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)

    _, c0 = _self_conv_fft(x, N)
    score0 = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best = x.copy()
    best_score = score0
    current = x.copy()
    current_score = score0

    if verbose and not _HAVE_LP:
        print("LP disabled; skipping LP improve.")

    target_coeff = max(float(target_score) / (2.0 * n), 1.0 / L)
    avg_coeff = 1.0 / L

    reduction = 0.004
    gamma = 0.25
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    if _HAVE_LP:
        max_nz = 1_200_000
        max_rows = max(25, min(int(top_rows) + int(random_rows), max(25, max_nz // max(1, n // 2 + 1))))
        if verbose:
            print(f"LP improve: n={n}, max rows~{max_rows}, target score={target_score:.6f}")
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if max_iters is not None and it >= max_iters:
                break
            X, c = _self_conv_fft(current, N)
            cmax = float(c.max())
            current_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
            if current_score < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_score = current_score
                best = current.copy()
                stall = 0

            if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, time_limit):
                break

            # Select important constraints: top peaks plus random spread.
            n_top = max(15, int(0.6 * max_rows))
            n_rand = min(max(0, max_rows - n_top), max(0, L // 20))
            if n_top >= L:
                top_idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
            else:
                top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -n_top)[-n_top:].astype(np.int64)
            if n_rand > 0:
                rand_idx = rng.integers(0, L, size=n_rand).astype(np.int64)
                selected = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, rand_idx)))
            else:
                selected = np.unique(top_idx)

            if selected.size < 15:
                break

            if cmax > target_coeff:
                M = max(target_coeff * 0.999, cmax * (1.0 - reduction))
            else:
                M = max(avg_coeff * 1.0005, cmax * 0.998)

            base_U = max(0.05, 10.0 * float(np.max(current)) + 1e-4)
            U_list = []
            for u in (base_U, min(2.0, 5.0 * base_U), 2.0):
                if u > 1e-8 and all(abs(u - v) > 1e-8 for v in U_list):
                    U_list.append(float(u))

            g = None
            for U_try in U_list:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                g = _lp_solve(current, selected, M, U_try, time_limit=time_limit)
                if g is not None and float(g.sum()) > 1e-10:
                    break

            if g is None:
                reduction = max(0.0008, reduction * 0.65)
                gamma = max(0.05, gamma * 0.9)
                stall += 1
                it += 1
                if stall > 8:
                    break
                continue

            g = _normalize_sum1(g)
            accepted = False
            for gz in (gamma, 0.5 * gamma, 0.1, 0.03):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                y = (1.0 - gz) * current + gz * g
                y = _normalize_sum1(y)
                _, cy = _self_conv_fft(y, N)
                sy = float(2.0 * n * cy.max())
                if sy < current_score - 1e-10:
                    current = y.copy()
                    current_score = sy
                    accepted = True
                    gamma = gz
                    break

            if accepted:
                reduction = min(0.04, reduction * 1.07 + 0.0004)
                stall = 0
                if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"  LP it {it}: accepted gamma={gz:.3f}, score={current_score:.6f}, best={best_score:.6f}")
            else:
                reduction = max(0.0008, reduction * 0.75)
                gamma = max(0.05, gamma * 0.85)
                stall += 1
                if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"  LP it {it}: rejected halts, reduction={reduction:.5f}, current={current_score:.6f}")
                if stall > 8:
                    break
            it += 1

    # Ensure last_x has finite sum.
    current = _normalize_sum1(current)
    return best, best_score, current


def _run_mixed(x, deadline, target_score=1.45, B_start=0.3, B_end=60.0,
               top_k=8, beta=0.85, lr=0.12, spec_interval=120,
               spec_max_red=0.7, min_lr=0.02, max_lr=0.60,
               rng=None, verbose=True):
    """
    Multiplicative smoothed subgradient descent plus occasional spectral
    square-root equalization. Returns (best_x, best_score, last_x).
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)

    x = _normalize_sum1(x)
    n = int(x.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)

    _, c0 = _self_conv_fft(x, N)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c0.max())
    best = x.copy()
    x_cur = x.copy()
    current_score = best_score

    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    B = float(B_start)
    spec_red = 0.02
    stuck = 0
    step = 0

    target_coeff = max(float(target_score) / (2.0 * n), 1.0 / L)
    avg_coeff = 1.0 / L

    if verbose:
        print(f"Mixed optimize start: n={n}, score={best_score:.6f}, target={float(target_score):.6f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        X, c = _self_conv_fft(x_cur, N)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if current_score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_score = current_score
            best = x_cur.copy()
            stuck = 0
            lr_cur = min(float(max_lr), lr_cur * 1.02)
            if verbose and (step % 250 == 0 or best_score < 1.51):
                print(f"  mixed step {step}: improved score={best_score:.6f}")

        # Occasional spectral equalization toward a capped target.
        skip_gradient = False
        if step > 20 and step % max(1, int(spec_interval)) == 0 and cmax > target_coeff * (1.0 + 1e-8):
            cap = max(target_coeff, cmax - spec_red * max(0.0, cmax - avg_coeff))
            T = _cap_target(c, cap)
            cand = _spectral_candidate(x_cur, X, T, N)
            accepted = False
            if cand is not None:
                _, cc = _self_conv_fft(cand, N)
                ccmax = float(cc.max())
                sc = float(2.0 * n * ccmax)
                if sc < current_score - 1e-10:
                    x_cur = cand.copy()
                    accepted = True
                    spec_red = min(float(spec_max_red), spec_red * 1.25)
                else:
                    blend = 0.70 * x_cur + 0.30 * cand
                    blend = _normalize_sum1(blend)
                    _, cb = _self_conv_fft(blend, N)
                    cbmax = float(cb.max())
                    sb = float(2.0 * n * cbmax)
                    if sb < current_score - 1e-10:
                        x_cur = blend.copy()
                        accepted = True
                        spec_red = min(float(spec_max_red), spec_red * 1.12)
                    else:
                        spec_red = max(0.004, spec_red * 0.82)
            if accepted:
                skip_gradient = True
                x_cur = _normalize_sum1(x_cur)
                # Do not evaluate same step further; next loop will recompute.
                step += 1
                continue

        if stuck > 0 and step % 25 == 0:
            lr_cur = max(float(min_lr), lr_cur * 0.985)

        if stuck > 900:
            # Reheat from best with proportional noise.
            noise_scale = 0.12 / max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(n)))
            x_cur = best + noise_scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            x_cur = _clean_array(x_cur)
            s = float(x_cur.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                x_cur = best.copy()
            else:
                x_cur = x_cur / s
            momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            B = max(float(B_start), B * 0.75)
            spec_red = 0.02
            stuck = 0
            lr_cur = max(float(min_lr), float(lr) * 0.7)

        # Anneal peak sharpness B.
        if step % 40 == 0:
            B = min(float(B_end), B * 1.09 + 0.05)

        # Gradient step.
        grad = _active_gradient(x_cur, c, int(top_k), B)
        g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if maxg > 1e-16:
            momentum = float(beta) * momentum + (1.0 - float(beta)) * g
            momentum = momentum - float(np.mean(momentum))
            maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(momentum)))
            if maxv > 1e-16:
                rel = (lr_cur / maxv) * momentum
                rel = np.clip(rel, -1.0, 1.0)
                x_cur = x_cur * np.exp(-rel)
                x_cur = np.maximum(x_cur, 1e-15)
                s = float(x_cur.sum())
                if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    x_cur = best.copy()
                else:
                    x_cur = x_cur / s
        else:
            # Very flat: small random kick.
            idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=max(5, n // 100))
            mul = 1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(idx.size)
            x_cur[idx] *= mul
            x_cur = _normalize_sum1(x_cur)

        step += 1

    current = _normalize_sum1(x_cur)
    return best, best_score, current


def _phase_deadline(frac, cap, total_deadline):
    """Deadline for a phase based on remaining budget and cap."""
    rem = max(0.0, float(total_deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem <= 5.0:
        return time.monotonic()
    dur = min(float(frac) * rem, float(cap))
    dur = max(1.0, dur)
    return time.monotonic() + dur


def _actual_score(seq):
    """Call the external evaluator safely."""
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        v = float(v)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return float("inf")
    return v


def _make_output(seq, n):
    """Scale a sum-1 sequence for final submission."""
    seq = _clean_array(seq)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s <= 1e-20:
        seq = np.full(max(1, int(n)), 1.0 / max(1, int(n)))
    else:
        seq = seq / s
    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, int(n))))
    out = seq * scale
    if out.size:
        m = float(np.max(out))
        if m > 999.0:
            out = out * (999.0 / m)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(x,x))/(sum(x)^2).

    Uses the available parent sequence if present, otherwise falls back to
    a parent-like length 5582 uniform start.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)

    # ---------------- Load / sanitize parent ----------------
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.reshape(-1)
        if p.size > 0:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
        n = 5582
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
        if parent.size > 8192:
            parent = _resample(parent, 8192)
        if parent.size < 64:
            n = 5582
            parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(parent.size)
    parent_norm = _normalize_sum1(parent)

    N = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    parent_score = _fast_score(parent_norm, N)
    if not np.isfinite(parent_score):
        parent_norm = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        parent_score = 2.0

    best = parent_norm.copy()
    best_n = n
    best_score = parent_score
    print(f"Initial parent: n={n}, internal score={parent_score:.6f}")

    # ---------------- Phase 1: LP-inspired coarse improvement ----------------
    if _HAVE_LP:
        dl = _phase_deadline(0.10, 120.0, total_deadline)
        if time.monotonic() < dl:
            b, sc, _ = _lp_improve(
                best, dl,
                target_score=max(1.45, best_score * 0.985),
                top_rows=180,
                random_rows=120,
                time_limit=3.0,
                rng=rng,
                verbose=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
                print(f"After LP phase1: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")
            else:
                print("LP phase1: no improvement accepted.")
    else:
        print("LP unavailable; continuing with gradient/spectral only.")

    # ---------------- Phase 2: main mixed optimization ----------------
    dl = _phase_deadline(0.35, 720.0, total_deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < dl:
        b, sc, _ = _run_mixed(
            best, dl,
            target_score=max(1.44, best_score * 0.98),
            B_start=0.25, B_end=45.0,
            top_k=8, beta=0.86, lr=0.12,
            spec_interval=140, spec_max_red=0.75,
            rng=rng, verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
            best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
            print(f"After mixed phase1: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")
        else:
            print("Mixed phase1: no improvement accepted.")

    # ---------------- Phase 3: second LP/Alien phase ----------------
    if _HAVE_LP:
        dl = _phase_deadline(0.08, 90.0, total_deadline)
        if time.monotonic() < dl:
            b, sc, _ = _lp_improve(
                best, dl,
                target_score=max(1.42, best_score * 0.985),
                top_rows=200,
                random_rows=120,
                time_limit=2.5,
                rng=rng,
                verbose=False
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
                print(f"After LP phase2: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")

    # ---------------- Phase 4: sharp fine gradient ----------------
    dl = _phase_deadline(0.25, 420.0, total_deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < dl:
        b, sc, _ = _run_mixed(
            best, dl,
            target_score=max(1.40, best_score * 0.985),
            B_start=12.0, B_end=130.0,
            top_k=3, beta=0.93, lr=0.045,
            spec_interval=500, spec_max_red=0.45,
            rng=rng, verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
            best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
            print(f"After fine mixed: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")

    # ---------------- Phase 5: localized restarts and optional longer length ----------------
    restart_fail = 0
    while time.monotonic() < total_deadline - 180 and restart_fail < 3:
        dl = _phase_deadline(0.30, 160.0, total_deadline)
        if dl <= time.monotonic():
            break
        sigma = 0.035 * (restart_fail + 1)
        z = best * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(best.size))
        z = _normalize_sum1(z)
        b, sc, _ = _run_mixed(
            z, dl,
            target_score=max(1.38, best_score * 0.99),
            B_start=8.0, B_end=90.0,
            top_k=3, beta=0.92, lr=0.035,
            spec_interval=350, spec_max_red=0.35,
            rng=rng, verbose=False
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
            best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
            restart_fail = 0
            print(f"Restart improved: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")
        else:
            restart_fail += 1

    # Try resampling to a larger length if time remains.
    if best_n < 8192 and time.monotonic() < total_deadline - 260:
        alt_n = 8192
        z = _resample(best, alt_n)
        z = z * np.exp(0.018 * rng.standard_normal(alt_n))
        z = _normalize_sum1(z)
        dl = _phase_deadline(0.45, 420.0, total_deadline)
        if time.monotonic() < dl:
            b, sc, _ = _run_mixed(
                z, dl,
                target_score=max(1.38, best_score * 0.99),
                B_start=1.0, B_end=60.0,
                top_k=6, beta=0.88, lr=0.09,
                spec_interval=200, spec_max_red=0.6,
                rng=rng, verbose=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best, best_score, best_n = b, sc, b.size
                print(f"Alt length improved: score={best_score:.6f} (n={best_n})")
            else:
                print("Alt length: no improvement accepted.")

    # ---------------- Final actual scoring of a few candidates ----------------
    candidates = []
    seen = set()

    def _add_candidate(seq, nn):
        key = (int(nn), float(seq.sum()) if seq is not None else -1.0)
        if key in seen:
            return
        if seq is None or seq.size != nn:
            return
        seen.add(key)
        candidates.append((seq.copy(), int(nn)))

    _add_candidate(best, best_n)
    _add_candidate(parent_norm, parent_norm.size)

    best_actual = float("inf")
    final_out = _make_output(best, best_n)

    for seq, nn in candidates:
        if seq.size == 0:
            continue
        out = _make_output(seq, nn)
        actual = _actual_score([float(v) for v in out])
        print(f"Candidate n={nn}: actual score={actual:.6f}")
        if actual < best_actual:
            best_actual = actual
            final_out = out

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(final_out)):
        final_out = _make_output(np.full(max(1, best_n), 1.0 / max(1, best_n)), best_n)

    print(f"Final selected score (actual): {best_actual:.6f}")
    return [float(v) for v in final_out]
```
